isinstance(aliases, list):\n        for a in aliases:\n            if isinstance(a, str) and a.strip():\n                labels.append(a.strip())\n\n    label_set = set(labels)\n    if not label_set:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Campaign {campaign.get('campaign_id')} has no labels to match billing\")\n\n    active_matches: List[Dict[str, str]] = []\n    for row in billing_rows:\n        row_label = (row.get(\"campaign_label\") or \"\").strip()\n        if row_label not in label_set:\n            continue\n        if (row.get(\"status\") or \"\").strip().lower() != \"active\":\n            continue\n        active_matches.append(row)\n\n    if not active_matches:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"No active billing rows found for campaign_id={campaign.get('campaign_id')} labels={sorted(label_set)}\"\n        )\n\n    # Latest = lexicographically greatest cycle_tag.\n    best = max(active_matches, key=lambda r: (r.get(\"cycle_tag\") or \"\"))\n    p = (best.get(\"payment_per_dispatch_per_clinic_usd\") or \"\").strip()\n    if not p:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"Billing row missing payment_per_dispatch_per_clinic_usd for campaign_id={campaign.get('campaign_id')}\"\n        )\n    return d(p)\n\n\ndef resolve_active_clinics_for_campaign(\n    campaign: Dict[str, Any],\n    override_rows: List[Dict[str, str]],\n) -> int:\n    cid = (campaign.get(\"campaign_id\") or \"\").strip()\n    if not cid:\n        raise ValueError(\"Manifest campaign missing campaign_id\")\n\n    best_rev: Optional[int] = None\n    best_active: Optional[int] = None\n\n    for row in override_rows:\n        if (row.get(\"campaign_id\") or \"\").strip() != cid:\n            continue\n\n        if (row.get(\"state\") or \"\").strip().lower() != \"approved\":\n            continue\n\n        rev_raw = (row.get(\"revision\") or \"\").strip()\n        active_raw = (row.get(\"active_clinics\") or \"\").strip()\n        if not rev_raw or not active_raw:\n            # Explicitly skip blank revision or blank active_clinics.\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            rev = int(float(rev_raw))\n        except ValueError:\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            active = int(float(active_raw))\n        except ValueError:\n            continue\n\n        if best_rev is None or rev > best_rev:\n            best_rev = rev\n            best_active = active\n\n    if best_active is not None:\n        return best_active\n\n    default_active = campaign.get(\"default_active_clinics\")\n    if default_active is None or str(default_active).strip() == \"\":\n        raise ValueError(f\"No active clinics override and default_active_clinics missing for {cid}\")\n    return int(float(default_active))\n\n\ndef load_crate_costs(crate_rows: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> Dict[str, Decimal]:\n    out: Dict[str, Decimal] = {}\n    for row in crate_rows:\n        tier = (row.get(\"crate_tier\") or \"\").strip()\n        cost = (row.get(\"crate_cost_usd\") or \"\").strip()\n        if not tier or not cost:\n            continue\n        out[tier] = d(cost)\n    return Any]:\n    drug_cost_per_1000 = d(campaign.get(\"drug_cost_per_1000_doses_usd\"))\n    doses_per_day = d(campaign.get(\"doses_per_day\"))\n\n    def annual_revenue(model: DispatchModel) -> Decimal:\n        return payment_per_dispatch_per_clinic_usd * d(active_clinics) * d(model.dispatches_per_year)\n\n    def annual_drug_cost(model: DispatchModel) -> Decimal:\n        return (\n            drug_cost_per_1000\n            * d(active_clinics)\n            * doses_per_day\n            * d(model.days_per_dispatch)\n            * d(model.dispatches_per_year)\n            / Decimal(\"1000\")\n        )\n\n    def annual_crate_cost(model: DispatchModel) -> Decimal:\n        # crate_cost_usd is per-dispatch; annualize by dispatches per year.\n        return crate_cost_usd * d(model.dispatches_per_year)\n\n    rev6 = annual_revenue(MODEL_6)\n    rev12 = annual_revenue(MODEL_12)\n\n    drug6 = annual_drug_cost(MODEL_6)\n    drug12 = annual_drug_cost(MODEL_12)\n\n    crate6 = annual_crate_cost(MODEL_6)\n    crate12 = annual_crate_cost(MODEL_12)\n\n    margin6 = rev6 - drug6 - crate6\n    margin12 = rev12 - drug12 - crate12\n    diff = margin12 - margin6\n\n    return {\n        \"campaign_id\": (campaign.get(\"campaign_id\") or \"\").strip(),\n        \"campaign_name\": (campaign.get(\"campaign_name\") or \"\").strip(),\n        \"active_clinics\": int(active_clinics),\n        \"crate_tier\": (campaign.get(\"crate_tier\") or \"\").strip(),\n        \"payment_per_dispatch_per_clinic_usd\": money(payment_per_dispatch_per_clinic_usd),\n        \"crate_cost_usd\": money(crate_cost_usd),\n        \"annual_revenue_6_day_usd\": money(rev6),\n        \"annual_revenue_12_day_usd\": money(rev12),\n        \"annual_drug_cost_6_day_usd\": money(drug6),\n        \"annual_drug_cost_12_day_usd\": money(drug12),\n        \"annual_crate_cost_6_day_usd\": money(crate6),\n        \"annual_crate_cost_12_day_usd\": money(crate12),\n None:\n    # 1) Load inputs.\n    manifest_obj = json.loads(MANIFEST_PATH.read_text())\n    manifest_campaigns = flatten_manifest_campaigns(manifest_obj)\n\n    crate_rows = \"\").strip() != \"review\":\n            continue\n        cid = (c.get(\"campaign_id\") or \"\").strip()\n        if cid in hold_ids:\n            continue\n        retained.append(c)\n\n    if not retained:\n        raise ValueError(\"No campaigns retained after filtering\")\n\n    # 5) Look up crate cost.\n    crate_cost_by_tier = load_crate_costs(crate_rows)\n\n    per_campaign: payment_per_dispatch_per_clinic_usd=payment,\n                active_clinics=active_clinics,\n                crate_cost_usd=crate_cost_usd,\n            )\n        )\n\n    # Sort campaigns by ${money(THRESHOLD_USD):,.2f}, so decision is '{decision}'.\"\n    )\n\n    # 9) Build JSON output.\n    out = {\n        \"assumptions\": {\n            \"models\": {\n                \"6_day\": {\n                    \"days_per_dispatch\": MODEL_6.days_per_dispatch,\n                    \"dispatches_per_year\": MODEL_6.dispatches_per_year,\n                },\n                \"12_day\": {\n                    \"days_per_dispatch\": MODEL_12.days_per_dispatch,\n                    \"dispatches_per_year\": MODEL_12.dispatches_per_year,\n                },\n            },\n            \"annual_crate_cost_interpretation\": \"crate_cost_usd is per dispatch; annual_crate_cost_usd = crate_cost_usd * dispatches_per_year\",\n
